                  [32m'\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\treturn fallback\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'func isS3NotFoundError(err error) bool {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tif err == nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\treturn false\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tvar apiErr smithy.APIError\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tif errors.As(err, &apiErr) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\tif apiErr.ErrorCode() == "NotFound" || apiErr.ErrorCode() == "NoSuchKey" {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\treturn true\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tvar responseError *awshttp.ResponseError\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tif errors.As(err, &responseError) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\tif responseError.ResponseError != nil && responseError.ResponseError.HTTPStatusCode() == http.StatusNotFound {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\treturn true\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\terrStr := err.Error()\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tif strings.Contains(errStr, "status code: 404") || strings.Contains(errStr, "NotFound") || strings.Contains(errStr, "NoSuchKey") {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\treturn true\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\treturn false\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'func ensureCollectionsExist(app core.App) error {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tusersCollection, err := app.FindCollectionByNameOrId("users")\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\tpendingUpload.Fields.Add(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t&core.RelationField{\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tName:         "user",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tRequired:     true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tCollectionId: usersCollection.Id,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tMaxSelect:    1,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t},\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t&core.TextField{\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tName:     "key",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tRequired: true,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t},\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t&core.DateField{\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tName:     "expires_at",\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\tif err := app.Save(pendingUpload); err != nil {\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t}\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t// 2. Check/create uploads\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t_, err = app.FindCollectionByNameOrId("uploads")\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\t\tName:         "user",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tRequired:     true,\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\t},\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\treturn nil\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'type FinalizeRequest struct {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tKey string `json:"key"`\n'[39m +
                  [32m'}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'func main() {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tapp := pocketbase.New()\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t// S3 Configuration\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\ts3Endpoint := getEnv("S3_ENDPOINT", "http://127.0.0.1:9000")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\ts3AccessKey := getEnv("S3_ACCESS_KEY", "minioadmin")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\ts3SecretKey := getEnv("S3_SECRET_KEY", "minioadmin")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\ts3Region := getEnv("S3_REGION", "us-east-1")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\ts3Bucket := getEnv("S3_BUCKET", "uploads")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tif err != nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\tlog.Fatalf("Failed to load S3 SDK configuration: %v", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\ts3Client := s3.NewFromConfig(cfg, func(o *s3.Options) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\to.BaseEndpoint = aws.String(s3Endpoint)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\to.UsePathStyle = true\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t})\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\tapp.OnBootstrap().BindFunc(func(e *core.BootstrapEvent) error {\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\treturn ensureCollectionsExist(e.App)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t})\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\tapp.OnServe().BindFunc(func(se *core.ServeEvent) error {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\tg := se.Router.Group("/api/uploads")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\tg.Bind(apis.RequireAuth("users"))\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t// POST /api/uploads/presign\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\tnow := time.Now()\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\texpiresAt := now.Add(300 * time.Second)\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.BadRequestError("Pending upload collection not found", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\tpendingRecord.Set("user", e.Auth.Id)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tpendingRecord.Set("key", key)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tpendingRecord.Set("expires_at", types.DateTime{Time: expiresAt})\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tif err := e.App.Save(pendingRecord); err != nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.BadRequestError("Failed to save pending upload record", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t// Generate S3 PUT presigned URL\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\t\tBucket: aws.String(s3Bucket),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tKey:    aws.String(key),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}, s3.WithPresignExpires(300 * time.Second))\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tif err != nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\t// Clean up the DB record if presign fails\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\t_ = e.App.Delete(pendingRecord)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.BadRequestError("Failed to generate presigned URL", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\treturn e.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\t"url":       presignedReq.URL,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\t"key":       key,\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\t"expiresAt": expiresAt.Format(time.RFC3339),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t})\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t})\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t// POST /api/uploads/finalize\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\tg.POST("/finalize", func(e *core.RequestEvent) error {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tif e.Auth == nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.UnauthorizedError("Unauthorized", nil)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tvar reqData FinalizeRequest\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tif err := e.BindBody(&reqData); err != nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.BadRequestError("Invalid request body", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tif reqData.Key == "" {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.BadRequestError("Missing key in request body", nil)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\tpendingRecord, err := e.App.FindFirstRecordByFilter(\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\t"pending_upload",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\t"key = {:key}",\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tdbx.Params{"key": reqData.Key},\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.NotFoundError("Pending upload belongs to a different user", nil)\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\t_, err = s3Client.HeadObject(e.Request.Context(), &s3.HeadObjectInput{\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tBucket: aws.String(s3Bucket),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tKey:    aws.String(reqData.Key),\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t})\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tif err != nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\tif isS3NotFoundError(err) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\t\treturn e.NotFoundError("Object not found in S3", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.BadRequestError("Failed to verify object existence in S3", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t// 3. Delete the pending_upload row\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tif err := e.App.Delete(pendingRecord); err != nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.BadRequestError("Failed to delete pending upload record", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t// 4. Create new uploads row\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tuploadsCollection, err := e.App.FindCollectionByNameOrId("uploads")\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tif err != nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.BadRequestError("Uploads collection not found", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tuploadRecord := core.NewRecord(uploadsCollection)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tuploadRecord.Set("user", e.Auth.Id)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tuploadRecord.Set("key", reqData.Key)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\tif err := e.App.Save(uploadRecord); err != nil {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t\treturn e.BadRequestError("Failed to save upload record", err)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\t}\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t\treturn e.JSON(http.StatusOK, map[string]any{\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\t\t})\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\t})\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t\treturn se.Next()\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\t})\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\t\tlog.Fatal(err)\n'[39m +

You must also consider if the command you need to run should be executed in a specific directory rather than the current working directory, and properly set the `cwd` if necessary.\n"[39m +
            [32m'\n'[39m +
            [32m'# Committing changes with git\n'[39m +
            [32m'\n'[39m +
            [32m'Only create commits when requested by the user. If unclear, ask first. When the user asks you to create a new git commit, follow these steps carefully:\n'[39m +
            [32m'\n'[39m +
            [32m'Git Safety Protocol:\n'[39m +
            [32m'- NEVER update the git config\n'[39m +
            [32m"- NEVER run destructive git commands (push --force, reset --hard, checkout ., restore ., clean -f, branch -D) unless the user explicitly requests these actions. Taking unauthorized destructive actions is unhelpful and can result in lost work, so it's best to ONLY run these commands when given direct instructions \n"[39m +
            [32m'- NEVER skip hooks (--no-verify, --no-gpg-sign, etc) unless the user explicitly requests it\n'[39m +
            [32m'- NEVER run force push to main/master, warn the user if they request it\n'[39m +
            [32m'- CRITICAL: Always create NEW commits rather than amending, unless the user explicitly requests a git amend. When a pre-commit hook fails, the commit did NOT happen  — so --amend would modify the PREVIOUS commit, which may result in destroying work or losing previous changes. Instead, after hook failure, fix the issue, re-stage, and create a NEW commit\n'[39m +
            [32m'- When staging files, prefer adding specific files by name rather than using "git add -A" or "git add .", which can accidentally include sensitive files (.env, credentials) or large binaries\n'[39m +
            [32m'- NEVER commit changes unless the user explicitly asks you to. It is VERY IMPORTANT to only commit when explicitly asked, otherwise the user will feel that you are being too proactive\n'[39m +
            [32m'\n'[39m +
            [32m'1. You can call multiple tools in a single response. When multiple independent pieces of information are requested and all commands are likely to succeed, run multiple tool calls in parallel for optimal performance. run the following bash commands in parallel, each using the executeCommand tool:\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Run a git status command to see all untracked files. IMPORTANT: Never use the -uall flag as it can cause memory issues on large repos.\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Run a git diff command to see both staged and unstaged changes that will be committed.\n'[39m +
            [32m"  - Run a git log command to see recent commit messages, so that you can follow this repository's commit message style.\n"[39m +
            [32m'2. Analyze all staged changes (both previously staged and newly added) and draft a commit message:\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Summarize the nature of the changes (eg. new feature, enhancement to an existing feature, bug fix, refactoring, test, docs, etc.). Ensure the message accurately reflects the changes and their purpose (i.e. "add" means a wholly new feature, "update" means an enhancement to an existing feature, "fix" means a bug fix, etc.).\n'[39m +
            [32m'  - Do not commit files that likely contain secrets (.env, credentials.json, etc). Warn the user if they specifically request to commit those files\n'[39m +
